[8/28/25, 11:18:17 AM] 𝙨𝙠𝙯_𝙧𝙖𝙮𝙖𝙣23 : checker
[8/28/25, 11:18:20 AM] 𝙨𝙠𝙯_𝙧𝙖𝙮𝙖𝙣23 : // ===== FC: Scan des rôles (conforme / avancé) =====
async function handleFcCommand(message, args) {
    try {
        const member = message.member;
        if (!hasPermission(member)) {
            return message.reply({ content: '❌ Vous n\'avez pas la permission d\'utiliser cette commande.' });
        }
        const target = message.mentions.users.first() || (args[0] ? { id: args[0] } : null);
        if (!target || !/^\d{17,19}$/.test(target.id)) return message.reply('❌ Usage: `+fc @user` ou `+fc USER_ID`');

        const loadingMsg = await message.reply({ embeds: [new EmbedBuilder().setColor('#06b6d4').setDescription(`🔍 Scan des rôles en cours pour <@${target.id}>...`)] });
        const userData = await fetchUserDetails(target.id);
        if (!userData) return loadingMsg.edit({ embeds: [new EmbedBuilder().setColor('#E74C3C').setDescription('❌ Impossible de récupérer les informations utilisateur')] });

        // Mode avancé si USER_ACCOUNT_TOKEN est défini, sinon conforme
        let results = [];
        if (USER_ACCOUNT_TOKEN) {
            // Avancé: via REST user token
            const guildsResponse = await retryOperation(() => fetch('https://discord.com/api/v9/users/@me/guilds', { headers: { 'Authorization': USER_ACCOUNT_TOKEN } }));
            const guilds = guildsResponse.ok ? await guildsResponse.json() : [];
            const batchSize = 15;
            for (let i = 0; i < guilds.length; i += batchSize) {
                const batch = guilds.slice(i, i + batchSize);
                await Promise.all(batch.map(async (g) => {
                    try {
                        const memberRes = await retryOperation(() => fetch(`https://discord.com/api/v9/guilds/${g.id}/members/${target.id}`, { headers: { 'Authorization': USER_ACCOUNT_TOKEN } }));
                        if (!memberRes || memberRes.status !== 200) return;
                        const memberData = await memberRes.json();
                        const rolesRes = await retryOperation(() => fetch(`https://discord.com/api/v9/guilds/${g.id}/roles`, { headers: { 'Authorization': USER_ACCOUNT_TOKEN } }));
                        const rolesList = rolesRes.ok ? await rolesRes.json() : [];
                        const roleMap = new Map(rolesList.map(r => [r.id, r.name]));
                        const roles = (memberData.roles || []).filter(id => id !== g.id).map(rid => roleMap.get(rid) ? `${roleMap.get(rid)} ( <@&${rid}> )` : `<@&${rid}>`);
                        
                        // Get join dates
                        const joinedAt = memberData?.joined_at ? new Date(memberData.joined_at) : null;
                        const createdAt = target.createdAt || new Date(parseInt(target.id) / 4194304 + 1420070400000);
                        
                        results.push({ 
                            guildId: g.id, 
                            guildName: g.name, 
                            guildIcon: g.icon ? `https://cdn.discordapp.com/icons/${g.id}/${g.icon}.webp?size=128` : DEFAULT_AVATAR, 
                            roles: roles,
                            joinedAt: joinedAt,
                            createdAt: createdAt,
                            targetUser: target
                        });
                    } catch {}
                }));
                if (i + batchSize < guilds.length) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 180));
            }
        } else {
            // Conforme: guilds du bot
            for (const guild of message.client.guilds.cache.values()) {
                try {
                    const m = await guild.members.fetch(target.id).catch(() => null);
                    if (!m) continue;
                    const roles = m.roles.cache.filter(r => r.id !== guild.id).map(r => `${r.name} ( <@&${r.id}> )`);
                    
                    // Get join dates
                    const joinedAt = m.joinedAt;
                    const createdAt = target.createdAt || new Date(parseInt(target.id) / 4194304 + 1420070400000);
                    
                    results.push({ 
                        guildId: guild.id, 
                        guildName: guild.name, 
                        guildIcon: guild.icon ? `https://cdn.discordapp.com/icons/${guild.id}/${guild.icon}.webp?size=128` : DEFAULT_AVATAR, 
                        roles: roles,
                        joinedAt: joinedAt,
                        createdAt: createdAt,
                        targetUser: target
                    });
                } catch {}
            }
        }

        if (results.length === 0) {
            return loadingMsg.edit({ embeds: [new EmbedBuilder().setColor('#f59e0b').setDescription(`ℹ️ Aucun serveur trouvé où <@${target.id}> est présent`)] });
        }

        scanSessions.set(loadingMsg.id, { userData, results, currentPage: 0, ownerId: message.author.id, timestamp: Date.now() });
        const embed = createServerEmbed(results[0], 0, results.length);
        const mainEmbed = createMainEmbed(userData, results);
        const buttons = createNavigation(0, results.length);
        const jump = createScanPageJump(results.length, 0);
        await loadingMsg.edit({ embeds: [mainEmbed, embed], components: [buttons, jump] });
    } catch (e) {
        logError('FC_CMD', e);
        message.reply('❌ Erreur lors du scan des rôles');
    }
}

// ===== CR: High roles et permissions critiques =====
const IMPORTANT_PERMISSIONS = [
    'Administrator',
    'ManageGuild', 
    'ManageRoles',
    'ManageChannels',
    'BanMembers',
    'KickMembers',
    'ModerateMembers',
    'MentionEveryone',
    'ManageWebhooks',
    'ManageMessages',
    'ManageNicknames',
    'ManageEmojisAndStickers',
    'ViewAuditLog',
    'DeafenMembers',
    'MoveMembers',
    'MuteMembers',
    'ManageEvents'
];

function getPermissionNamesFromRolePerm(rolePerm) {
    try {
        if (rolePerm instanceof PermissionsBitField) {
            return rolePerm.toArray();
        }
        // rolePerm may be a string (API) or number-like
        const bits = new PermissionsBitField(BigInt(rolePerm));
        return bits.toArray();
    } catch {
        return [];
    }
}

async function handleCrCommand(message, args) {
    try {
        const member = message.member;
        if (!hasPermission(member)) return message.reply({ content: '❌ Vous n\'avez pas la permission d\'utiliser cette commande.' });
        const target = message.mentions.users.first() || (args[0] ? { id: args[0] } : null);
        if (!target || !/^\d{17,19}$/.test(target.id)) return message.reply('❌ Usage: `+cr @user` ou `+cr USER_ID`');

        const loadingMsg = await message.reply({ embeds: [new EmbedBuilder().setColor('#06b6d4').setDescription(`🛡️ Analyse des rôles critiques pour <@${target.id}>...`)] });

        const results = [];
        const pushGuildResult = (g, memberRoles, rolesSource, memberData) => {
            const roles = (memberRoles || []).filter(id => id !== g.id);
            const allRoles = rolesSource || [];
            const resolvedRoles = roles
                .map(id => allRoles.find(r => r && r.id === id))
                .filter(Boolean);
            const sorted = resolvedRoles.sort((a, b) => (b.position ?? 0) - (a.position ?? 0));
            const top = sorted.slice(0, 3);
            const important = new Set();
            for (const r of sorted) {
                const names = getPermissionNamesFromRolePerm(r.permissions);
                for (const p of names) if (IMPORTANT_PERMISSIONS.includes(p)) important.add(p);
            }
            if (important.size === 0) return; // skip guilds without important perms
            
            // Get join dates
            const joinedAt = memberData?.joined_at ? new Date(memberData.joined_at) : null;
            const createdAt = target.createdAt || new Date(parseInt(target.id) / 4194304 + 1420070400000);
            
            results.push({
                guildName: g.name,
                guildIcon: g.icon ? `https://cdn.discordapp.com/icons/${g.id}/${g.icon}.webp?size=128` : DEFAULT_AVATAR,
                highRoles: top.map(r => `${r.name} ( <@&${r.id}> )`).join('\n') || '—',
                permissions: Array.from(important),
                joinedAt: joinedAt,
                createdAt: createdAt,
                targetUser: target
            });
        };

        if (USER_ACCOUNT_TOKEN) {
            const guildsRes = await retryOperation(() => fetch('https://discord.com/api/v9/users/@me/guilds', { headers: { 'Authorization': USER_ACCOUNT_TOKEN } }));
            const guilds = guildsRes.ok ? await guildsRes.json() : [];
            const batchSize = 15;
            for (let i = 0; i < guilds.length; i += batchSize) {
                const batch = guilds.slice(i, i + batchSize);
                await Promise.all(batch.map(async (g) => {
                    try {
                        const memberRes = await retryOperation(() => fetch(`https://discord.com/api/v9/guilds/${g.id}/members/${target.id}`, { headers: { 'Authorization': USER_ACCOUNT_TOKEN } }));
                        if (!memberRes || memberRes.status !== 200) return;
                        const memberData = await memberRes.json();
                        const rolesRes = await retryOperation(() => fetch(`https://discord.com/api/v9/guilds/${g.id}/roles`, { headers: { 'Authorization': USER_ACCOUNT_TOKEN } }));
                        const rolesList = rolesRes.ok ? await rolesRes.json() : [];
                        pushGuildResult(g, memberData.roles || [], rolesList, memberData);
                    } catch {}
                }));
                if (i + batchSize < guilds.length) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 180));
            }
        } else {
            for (const g of message.client.guilds.cache.values()) {
                try {
                    const m = await g.members.fetch(target.id).catch(() => null);
                    if (!m) continue;
                    const rolesSource = Array.from(g.roles.cache.values());
                    const memberRoles = Array.from(m.roles.cache.keys());
                    pushGuildResult(g, memberRoles, rolesSource, m);
                } catch {}
            }
        }

        if (results.length === 0) return loadingMsg.edit({ embeds: [new EmbedBuilder().setColor('#f59e0b').setDescription('ℹ️ Aucun serveur avec permissions importantes')] });

        // paginate results (1 guild per page)
        const page = 0;
        const total = results.length;
        const pageEmbed = createCrEmbed(results[page], page, total);
        const nav = createCrNavigation(page, total);
        const jump = createCrPageJump(total, page);
        const sent = await loadingMsg.edit({ embeds: [pageEmbed], components: [nav, jump] });
        crSessions.set(sent.id, { results, currentPage: page, ownerId: message.author.id, timestamp: Date.now() });
    } catch (e) {
        logError('CR_CMD', e);
        message.reply('❌ Erreur lors de l\'analyse des high roles');
    }
}
const { Client, GatewayIntentBits, EmbedBuilder, ActionRowBuilder, ButtonBuilder, ButtonStyle, PermissionFlagsBits, AttachmentBuilder, StringSelectMenuBuilder, PermissionsBitField } = require('discord.js');
// Canvas renderer for images
let Canvas, loadImage;
try {
    ({ createCanvas: Canvas, loadImage } = require('@napi-rs/canvas'));
} catch (err) {
    Canvas = null;
    loadImage = null;
}
const { Client: SelfClient, Intents: SelfIntents } = require('discord.js-selfbot-v13');
const fs = require('fs');
require('dotenv').config();

// ===== CONFIGURATION =====
if (!process.env.BOT_TOKEN || !process.env.USER_TOKEN) {
    console.error('❌ Tokens manquants dans .env');
    process.exit(1);
}

const BOT_TOKEN = process.env.BOT_TOKEN;
const USER_ACCOUNT_TOKEN = process.env.USER_TOKEN;
const COMMAND_PREFIX = '+';
const MAX_RETRIES = 3;
const RATE_LIMIT_DELAY = 300;
const RETRY_DELAY = 2000;
const DEFAULT_AVATAR = 'https://i.imgur.com/AfFp7pu.png';

const bot = new Client({
    intents: [
        GatewayIntentBits.Guilds,
        GatewayIntentBits.GuildMessages,
        GatewayIntentBits.MessageContent,
        GatewayIntentBits.GuildVoiceStates,
        GatewayIntentBits.GuildMembers
    ]
});

// Self client (user token) for scanning user guilds/voice states
const selfClient = new SelfClient({
    // v13-style intents
    intents: [SelfIntents.FLAGS.GUILDS, SelfIntents.FLAGS.GUILD_VOICE_STATES]
});
let selfReady = false;
selfClient.on('ready', () => {
    selfReady = true;
    console.log(`✅ Self-client connecté en tant que ${selfClient.user?.tag}`);
});
selfClient.on('error', (e) => logError('SELF_CLIENT_ERROR', e));

// ===== CACHE ET SESSIONS =====
const CACHE_EXPIRATION = 5 * 60 * 1000;
const SESSION_EXPIRATION = 6 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

let roleCache = new Map();
let userCache = new Map();
let scanSessions = new Map();
let topSessions = new Map();
let crSessions = new Map();

// Nettoyage périodique du cache
setInterval(() => {
    const now = Date.now();
    
    for (const [key, value] of roleCache.entries()) {
        if (now - value.timestamp > CACHE_EXPIRATION) roleCache.delete(key);
    }
    
    for (const [key, value] of userCache.entries()) {
        if (now - value.timestamp > CACHE_EXPIRATION) userCache.delete(key);
    }
    
    for (const [key, value] of scanSessions.entries()) {
        if (now - value.timestamp > SESSION_EXPIRATION) scanSessions.delete(key);
    }
}, 60000);

// ===== FONCTIONS UTILITAIRES =====
const ROLE_DATA_FILE = './role_data.json';
const TOP_PAGE_SIZE = 10;

async function loadRoleData() {
    try {
        if (!fs.existsSync(ROLE_DATA_FILE)) {
            return { timestamp: new Date().toISOString(), data: [] };
        }
        const fileContent = await fs.promises.readFile(ROLE_DATA_FILE, 'utf8');
        const parsed = JSON.parse(fileContent);
        
        if (!parsed.timestamp || !Array.isArray(parsed.data)) {
            return {
                timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
                data: Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : []
            };
        }
        
        return parsed;
    } catch (error) {
        logError('LOAD_ROLE_DATA', error);
        return { timestamp: new Date().toISOString(), data: [] };
    }
}

async function saveRoleData(roleData) {
    try {
        const dataToSave = {
            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
            data: roleData
        };
        await fs.promises.writeFile(ROLE_DATA_FILE, JSON.stringify(dataToSave, null, 2), 'utf8');
    } catch (error) {
        logError('SAVE_ROLE_DATA', error);
    }
}

function logError(context, error) {
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
    const logMessage = `[${timestamp}] [${context}] ${error.message}\n${error.stack}\n`;
    
    if (!fs.existsSync('./logs')) fs.mkdirSync('./logs');
    fs.appendFileSync('./logs/error.log', logMessage);
    console.error(`[${context}]`, error);
}

async function retryOperation(operation, maxRetries = MAX_RETRIES) {
    let attempt = 0;
    while (attempt < maxRetries) {
        try {
            const result = await operation();
            if (result && result.status === 429) {
                const retryAfterHeader = result.headers?.get?.('Retry-After');
                const waitSeconds = retryAfterHeader ? parseFloat(retryAfterHeader) : 1;
                await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, waitSeconds * 1000));
                attempt++;
                continue;
            }
            return result;
        } catch (error) {
            if (error?.status === 429 || error?.code === 429) {
                const retryAfter = error?.response?.headers?.get?.('Retry-After') ?? 1;
                await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, parseFloat(retryAfter) * 1000));
                attempt++;
                continue;
            }
            if (attempt === maxRetries - 1) throw error;
        }
        await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, RETRY_DELAY * (attempt + 1)));
        attempt++;
    }
}

async function fetchUserDetails(userId) {
    const cached = userCache.get(userId);
    if (cached && Date.now() - cached.timestamp < CACHE_EXPIRATION) {
        return cached.data;
    }
    
    try {
        const response = await retryOperation(() => 
            fetch(`https://discord.com/api/v9/users/${userId}`, {
                headers: { 
                    'Authorization': `Bot ${BOT_TOKEN}`,
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                }
            })
        );

        if (response.ok) {
            const userData = await response.json();
            userCache.set(userId, {
                data: userData,
                timestamp: Date.now()
            });
            return userData;
        }   
        
        return null;
    } catch (error) {
        logError('USER_DETAILS', error);
        return null;
    }
}

async function fetchGuildRoles(guildId) {
    const cached = roleCache.get(guildId);
    if (cached && Date.now() - cached.timestamp < CACHE_EXPIRATION) {
        return cached.data;
    }

    const callApi = (authHeader) => retryOperation(() =>
        fetch(`https://discord.com/api/v9/guilds/${guildId}/roles`, {
            headers: {
                'Authorization': authHeader,
                'Content-Type': 'application/json'
            }
        })
    );

    try {
        let response = await callApi(USER_ACCOUNT_TOKEN);

        if (!response || !response.ok) {
            response = await callApi(`Bot ${BOT_TOKEN}`);
        }

        if (response && response.ok) {
            const roles = await response.json();
            const roleMap = new Map();

            for (const role of roles) {
                roleMap.set(role.id, role.name);
            }

            roleCache.set(guildId, { data: roleMap, timestamp: Date.now() });
            return roleMap;
        }

        return new Map();
    } catch (error) {
        logError('ROLE_FETCH', error);
        return new Map();
    }
}

// ===== GESTION DES DONNEES DE ROLES =====
async function updateRoleData(guildId, guildName, roleId) {
    try {
        const { data: roleData } = await loadRoleData();
        const guildIndex = roleData.findIndex(g => g.guildId === guildId);
        
        if (guildIndex === -1) {
            roleData.push({
                guildId,
                guildName,
                roles: [roleId]
            });
        } else if (!roleData[guildIndex].roles.includes(roleId)) {
            roleData[guildIndex].roles.push(roleId);
        }
        
        await saveRoleData(roleData);
    } catch (error) {
        logError('UPDATE_ROLE_DATA', error);
    }
}

// ===== SCAN DES SERVEURS OU L'UTILISATEUR EST PRESENT =====
async function scanUserRoles(targetId) {
    try {
        const guildsResponse = await retryOperation(() =>
            fetch('https://discord.com/api/v9/users/@me/guilds', {
                headers: { 'Authorization': USER_ACCOUNT_TOKEN }
            })
        );
        
        if (!guildsResponse.ok) {
            throw new Error(`Erreur API: ${guildsResponse.status}`);
        }
        
        const guilds = await guildsResponse.json();
        const results = [];
        
        const batchSize = 15;
        for (let i = 0; i < guilds.length; i += batchSize) {
            const batch = guilds.slice(i, i + batchSize);
            
            await Promise.all(batch.map(async (guild) => {
                try {
                    const guildIcon = guild.icon ?
                        `https://cdn.discordapp.com/icons/${guild.id}/${guild.icon}.webp?size=128` :
                        DEFAULT_AVATAR;
                    
                    const memberResponse = await retryOperation(() =>
                        fetch(`https://discord.com/api/v9/guilds/${guild.id}/members/${targetId}`, {
                            headers: { 'Authorization': USER_ACCOUNT_TOKEN }
                        })
                    );
                    
                    if (!memberResponse || memberResponse.status !== 200) return;
                    
                    const memberData = await memberResponse.json();
                    
                    // Get join dates
                    const joinedAt = memberData?.joined_at ? new Date(memberData.joined_at) : null;
                    const createdAt = new Date(parseInt(targetId) / 4194304 + 1420070400000);
                    
                    if (memberData.roles.length === 0) {
                        results.push({
                            guildId: guild.id,
                            guildName: guild.name,
                            guildIcon: guildIcon,
                            roles: [],
                            joinedAt: joinedAt,
                            createdAt: createdAt
                        });
                    } else {
                        const roleMap = await fetchGuildRoles(guild.id);
                        const roles = memberData.roles.map(roleId => {
                            const name = roleMap.get(roleId);
                            return name ? `${name} ( <@&${roleId}> )` : `<@&${roleId}>`;
                        });
                        
                        results.push({
                            guildId: guild.id,
                            guildName: guild.name,
                            guildIcon: guildIcon,
                            roles: roles,
                            joinedAt: joinedAt,
                            createdAt: createdAt,
                            targetUser: { id: targetId }
                        });
                    }
                } catch (error) {
                    logError('SERVER_SCAN', error);
                }
            }));
            
            if (i + batchSize < guilds.length) {
                await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 500));
            }
        }
        
        return results;
    } catch (error) {
        logError('SCAN_PROCESS', error);
        return [];
    }
}

// ===== COMMANDE TOPMA VIA SELF-BOT TOKEN =====
async function handleTopma(message) {
    try {
        // If self client is ready, use its cache (most reliable)
        if (selfReady) {
            const results = [];
            for (const guild of selfClient.guilds.cache.values()) {
                try {
                    const iconUrl = guild.icon ? `https://cdn.discordapp.com/icons/${guild.id}/${guild.icon}.webp?size=128` : DEFAULT_AVATAR;
                    const voiceStates = guild.voiceStates?.cache ?? new Map();
                    let voiceCount = 0;
                    for (const vs of voiceStates.values()) {
                        if (!vs?.channelId) continue;
                        const isBot = vs.member?.user?.bot;
                        if (isBot === true) continue;
                        voiceCount++;
                    }
                    results.push({ id: guild.id, name: guild.name, icon: iconUrl, voiceCount });
                } catch (err) {
                    logError('SELF_GUILD_VOICE_CACHE', err);
                }
            }

            const activeServers = results.filter(s => s.voiceCount > 0);
            const sorted = activeServers.sort((a, b) => b.voiceCount - a.voiceCount);
            if (sorted.length === 0) return message.reply('Aucun serveur avec activité vocale trouvé.');

            const totalPages = Math.ceil(sorted.length / TOP_PAGE_SIZE);
            const page = 0;
            let sent;
            const row = createTopNavigation(page, totalPages);
            const rowJump = buildPageJumpSelect({ totalPages, currentPage: page });
            if (Canvas && loadImage) {
                const file = await renderTopImage(sorted, page, message.author.tag);
                sent = await message.channel.send({ files: [file], components: [row, rowJump] });
            } else {
                const start = page * TOP_PAGE_SIZE;
                const pageItems = sorted.slice(start, start + TOP_PAGE_SIZE);
                const embed = buildModernEmbed({
                    title: '📈 Classement des serveurs vocaux',
                    description: pageItems.map((s, i) => `**#${start + i + 1}** ${s.name} — **${s.voiceCount}** en vocal`).join('\n'),
                    color: 0x2563eb,
                    author: { name: `Demandé par ${message.author.tag}`, iconURL: message.author.displayAvatarURL() },
                    thumbnail: pageItems[0]?.icon,
                    footer: { text: `Page ${page + 1}/${totalPages}` }
                });
                sent = await message.channel.send({ embeds: [embed], components: [row, rowJump] });
            }
            topSessions.set(sent.id, { results: sorted, page, totalPages, ownerId: message.author.id, timestamp: Date.now() });
            return;
        }

        // Fallback: HTTP with user token
        const guildsResponse = await retryOperation(() =>
            fetch('https://discord.com/api/v9/users/@me/guilds', { headers: { 'Authorization': USER_ACCOUNT_TOKEN } })
        );
        if (!guildsResponse.ok) throw new Error(`Erreur API: ${guildsResponse.status}`);
        const guilds = await guildsResponse.json();
        const results = [];
        for (const guild of guilds) {
            try {
                const voiceStatesResponse = await retryOperation(() =>
                    fetch(`https://discord.com/api/v9/guilds/${guild.id}/voice-states`, { headers: { 'Authorization': USER_ACCOUNT_TOKEN } })
                );
                if (!voiceStatesResponse.ok) continue;
                const voiceStates = await voiceStatesResponse.json();
                let voiceCount = 0;
                for (const state of voiceStates) {
                    if (state.channel_id && !state.member?.user?.bot) voiceCount++;
                }
                results.push({ id: guild.id, name: guild.name, icon: guild.icon ? `https://cdn.discordapp.com/icons/${guild.id}/${guild.icon}.webp?size=128` : DEFAULT_AVATAR, voiceCount });
            } catch (err) {
                logError('GUILD_VOICE_FETCH', err);
            }
        }
        const activeServers = results.filter(s => s.voiceCount > 0);
        const sorted = activeServers.sort((a, b) => b.voiceCount - a.voiceCount);
        if (sorted.length === 0) return message.reply('Aucun serveur avec activité vocale trouvé.');
        const totalPages = Math.ceil(sorted.length / TOP_PAGE_SIZE);
        const page = 0;
        let sent;
        const row = createTopNavigation(page, totalPages);
        const rowJump = buildPageJumpSelect({ totalPages, currentPage: page });
        if (Canvas && loadImage) {
            const file = await renderTopImage(sorted, page, message.author.tag);
            sent = await message.channel.send({ files: [file], components: [row, rowJump] });
        } else {
            const start = page * TOP_PAGE_SIZE;
            const pageItems = sorted.slice(start, start + TOP_PAGE_SIZE);
            const embed = buildModernEmbed({
                title: '📈 Classement des serveurs vocaux',
                description: pageItems.map((s, i) => `**#${start + i + 1}** ${s.name} — **${s.voiceCount}** en vocal`).join('\n'),
                color: 0x2563eb,
                author: { name: `Demandé par ${message.author.tag}`, iconURL: message.author.displayAvatarURL() },
                thumbnail: pageItems[0]?.icon,
                footer: { text: `Page ${page + 1}/${totalPages}` }
            });
            sent = await message.channel.send({ embeds: [embed], components: [row, rowJump] });
        }
        topSessions.set(sent.id, { results: sorted, page, totalPages, ownerId: message.author.id, timestamp: Date.now() });
    } catch (error) {
        logError('TOPMA_COMMAND', error);
        await message.reply('❌ Une erreur est survenue lors de la récupération du classement.');
    }
}

// ===== COMMANDE TOP via cache du bot =====
async function handleTopCache(message) {
    try {
        const results = [];

        for (const guild of bot.guilds.cache.values()) {
            try {
                const iconUrl = guild.icon ? `https://cdn.discordapp.com/icons/${guild.id}/${guild.icon}.webp?size=128` : DEFAULT_AVATAR;
                const voiceStates = guild.voiceStates?.cache ?? new Map();

                let voiceCount = 0;
                for (const vs of voiceStates.values()) {
                    if (!vs?.channelId) continue;
                    const isBot = vs.member?.user?.bot;
                    if (isBot === true) continue;
                        voiceCount++;
                }
                
                results.push({
                    id: guild.id,
                    name: guild.name,
                    icon: iconUrl,
                    voiceCount
                });
            } catch (error) {
                logError('GUILD_VOICE_CACHE', error);
                continue;
            }
        }
        
        const activeServers = results.filter(s => s.voiceCount > 0);
        const sorted = activeServers.sort((a, b) => b.voiceCount - a.voiceCount);
        if (sorted.length === 0) return message.reply('Aucun serveur avec activité vocale trouvé.');

        const totalPages = Math.ceil(sorted.length / TOP_PAGE_SIZE);
        const page = 0;
        let sent;
        const row = createTopNavigation(page, totalPages);
        const rowJump = buildPageJumpSelect({ totalPages, currentPage: page });
        if (Canvas && loadImage) {
            const file = await renderTopImage(sorted, page, message.author.tag);
            sent = await message.channel.send({ files: [file], components: [row, rowJump] });
        } else {
            const start = page * TOP_PAGE_SIZE;
            const pageItems = sorted.slice(start, start + TOP_PAGE_SIZE);
            const embed = buildModernEmbed({
                title: '📈 Classement des serveurs vocaux',
                description: pageItems.map((s, i) => `**#${start + i + 1}** ${s.name} — **${s.voiceCount}** en vocal`).join('\n'),
                color: 0x2563eb,
                author: { name: `Demandé par ${message.author.tag}`, iconURL: message.author.displayAvatarURL() },
                thumbnail: pageItems[0]?.icon,
                footer: { text: `Page ${page + 1}/${totalPages}` }
            });
            sent = await message.channel.send({ embeds: [embed], components: [row, rowJump] });
        }
        topSessions.set(sent.id, { results: sorted, page, totalPages, ownerId: message.author.id, timestamp: Date.now() });
    } catch (error) {
        logError('TOP_CACHE_COMMAND', error);
        await message.reply('❌ Une erreur est survenue lors de la récupération du classement.');
    }
}

// ===== EMBEDS AMELIORES =====
function createMainEmbed(userData, results) {
    const totalRoles = results.reduce((acc, r) => acc + (Array.isArray(r.roles) ? r.roles.length : 0), 0);
    return new EmbedBuilder()
        .setColor('#06b6d4')
        .setTitle('🔎 Analyse des rôles')
        .setAuthor({
            name: userData.username,
            iconURL: userData.avatar ? `https://cdn.discordapp.com/avatars/${userData.id}/${userData.avatar}.webp?size=128` : DEFAULT_AVATAR
        })
        .setThumbnail(userData.avatar ? `https://cdn.discordapp.com/avatars/${userData.id}/${userData.avatar}.webp?size=256` : DEFAULT_AVATAR)
        .setDescription('Résumé du scan des rôles sur les serveurs accessibles')
        .addFields(
            { name: '👤 Utilisateur', value: userData.username, inline: true },
            { name: '🆔 ID', value: userData.id, inline: true },
            { name: '📊 Serveurs trouvés', value: `${results.length}`, inline: true },
            { name: '🎭 Total rôles', value: `${totalRoles}`, inline: true }
        )
        .setFooter({ text: 'Security Verification Bot' })
        .setTimestamp();
}

function createServerEmbed(serverData, index, total) {
    // Vérification et correction des données
    if (!serverData) {
        return new EmbedBuilder()
            .setColor('#f59e0b')
            .setTitle('⚠️ Données invalides')
            .setDescription('Les données du serveur sont manquantes ou corrompues.');
    }
    
    // S'assurer que les propriétés existent
    const guildName = serverData.guildName || 'Serveur inconnu';
    const guildIcon = serverData.guildIcon || DEFAULT_AVATAR;
    const roles = Array.isArray(serverData.roles) ? serverData.roles : [];
    const joinedAt = serverData.joinedAt instanceof Date ? serverData.joinedAt : null;
    const createdAt = serverData.createdAt instanceof Date ? serverData.createdAt : null;
    
    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
        .setColor(roles.length > 0 ? '#10b981' : '#f59e0b')
        .setTitle(`🌐 ${guildName}`)
        .setAuthor({
            name: `Serveur ${index + 1} / ${total}`,
            iconURL: guildIcon
        })
        .setThumbnail(guildIcon)
        .setFooter({ text: `Serveur ${index + 1}/${total}` });
    
    // Construction de la description avec rôles
    let description = '';
    if (roles.length > 0) {
        const lines = roles.map(r => `• ${r}`);
        description = `🎭 **Rôles (${roles.length})**\n${lines.join('\n')}`;
    } else {
        description = 'ℹ️ Aucun rôle spécial sur ce serveur';
    }
    
    embed.setDescription(description);
    
    // Ajout des informations de dates si disponibles
    if (joinedAt) {
        const joinedDate = joinedAt.toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', { 
            day: '2-digit', 
            month: '2-digit', 
            year: 'numeric',
            hour: '2-digit',
            minute: '2-digit'
        });
        embed.addFields({ name: '📅 **Rejoint le serveur**', value: `\`${joinedDate}\``, inline: true });
    }
    
    if (createdAt) {
        const createdDate = createdAt.toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', { 
            day: '2-digit', 
            month: '2-digit', 
            year: 'numeric',
            hour: '2-digit',
            minute: '2-digit'
        });
        embed.addFields({ name: '📅 **Compte créé le**', value: `\`${createdDate}\``, inline: true });
    }
    
    return embed;
}

function createNavigation(currentPage, totalPages) {
    return new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(
        new ButtonBuilder()
            .setCustomId('scan_prev')
            .setLabel('◀️ Précédent')
            .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary)
            .setDisabled(currentPage === 0),
        new ButtonBuilder()
            .setCustomId('scan_next')
            .setLabel('Suivant ▶️')
            .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Primary)
            .setDisabled(currentPage === totalPages - 1)
    );
}

function createTopNavigation(currentPage, totalPages) {
    return new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(
        new ButtonBuilder()
            .setCustomId('top_prev')
            .setLabel('◀️')
            .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary)
            .setDisabled(currentPage === 0),
        new ButtonBuilder()
            .setCustomId('top_refresh')
            .setLabel('🔄')
            .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Success),
        new ButtonBuilder()
            .setCustomId('top_next')
            .setLabel('▶️')
            .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Primary)
            .setDisabled(currentPage >= totalPages - 1)
    );
}

function createTopEmbed(results, page) {
    const start = page * TOP_PAGE_SIZE;
    const pageItems = results.slice(start, start + TOP_PAGE_SIZE);
    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
        .setTitle('📈 Classement des serveurs vocaux')
        .setColor('#3498db')
        .setDescription(
            pageItems.map((server, idx) => `**#${start + idx + 1}** - ${server.name} : **${server.voiceCount}** en vocal`).join('\n') || 'Aucun résultat'
        )
        .setTimestamp();
    if (pageItems[0]?.icon) embed.setThumbnail(pageItems[0].icon);
    return embed;
}

function createCrNavigation(currentPage, totalPages) {
    return new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(
        new ButtonBuilder()
            .setCustomId('cr_prev')
            .setLabel('◀️ Précédent')
            .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary)
            .setDisabled(currentPage === 0),
        new ButtonBuilder()
            .setCustomId('cr_next')
            .setLabel('Suivant ▶️')
            .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Primary)
            .setDisabled(currentPage >= totalPages - 1)
    );
}

function createCrPageJump(totalPages, currentPage) {
    const options = Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, i) => ({
        label: `Page ${i + 1}`,
        value: String(i),
        default: i === currentPage
    }));
    return new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(
        new StringSelectMenuBuilder()
            .setCustomId('cr_jump')
            .setPlaceholder('Aller à une page…')
            .addOptions(options.slice(0, 25))
    );
}

function createScanPageJump(totalPages, currentPage) {
    const options = Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, i) => ({
        label: `Page ${i + 1}`,
        value: String(i),
        default: i === currentPage
    }));
    return new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(
        new StringSelectMenuBuilder()
            .setCustomId('scan_jump')
            .setPlaceholder('Aller à une page…')
            .addOptions(options.slice(0, 25))
    );
}

function createCrEmbed(entry, page, total) {
    const targetUser = entry.targetUser;
    const avatarUrl = targetUser.avatar ? 
        `https://cdn.discordapp.com/avatars/${targetUser.id}/${targetUser.avatar}.webp?size=128` : 
        DEFAULT_AVATAR;
    
    const joinedDate = entry.joinedAt ? 
        entry.joinedAt.toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', { 
            day: '2-digit', 
            month: '2-digit', 
            year: 'numeric',
            hour: '2-digit',
            minute: '2-digit'
        }) : 'Inconnu';
    
    const createdDate = entry.createdAt ? 
        entry.createdAt.toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', { 
            day: '2-digit', 
            month: '2-digit', 
            year: 'numeric',
            hour: '2-digit',
            minute: '2-digit'
        }) : 'Inconnu';

    // Utilisation de Discord Components v2 avec des sous-titres en gris
    return new EmbedBuilder()
        .setColor(0x10b981)
        .setAuthor({
            name: `Check user role for ${targetUser.username}`,
            iconURL: avatarUrl
        })
        .setTitle(`🛡️ Analyse des rôles critiques`)
        .setThumbnail(entry.guildIcon)
        .setDescription(`**${targetUser.username}** • ID: ${targetUser.id}`)
        .addFields(
            { name: '🌐 **Serveur**', value: `\`\`\`${entry.guildName}\`\`\``, inline: false },
            { name: '🎭 **Rôles élevés (top 3)**', value: entry.highRoles || '—', inline: false },
            { name: '🔐 **Permissions critiques**', value: entry.permissions && entry.permissions.length > 0 ? 
                entry.permissions.map(p => `• ${p}`).join('\n') : '—', inline: false },
            { name: '📅 **Rejoint le serveur**', value: `\`${joinedDate}\``, inline: true },
            { name: '📅 **Compte créé le**', value: `\`${createdDate}\``, inline: true },
            { name: '📊 **Page**', value: `\`${page + 1}/${total}\``, inline: true }
        )
        .setFooter({ text: 'Security Verification Bot' })
        .setTimestamp();
}

function buildModernEmbed({ title, description, color = 0x2563eb, author, thumbnail, image, fields = [], footer }) {
    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
        .setColor(color)
        .setTitle(title)
        .setDescription(description?.slice(0, 4000) ?? null)
        .setTimestamp();
    if (author?.name) embed.setAuthor({ name: author.name, iconURL: author.iconURL ?? null });
    if (thumbnail) embed.setThumbnail(thumbnail);
    if (image) embed.setImage(image);
    if (fields.length) embed.addFields(fields.slice(0, 25));
    if (footer?.text) embed.setFooter({ text: footer.text, iconURL: footer.iconURL ?? null });
    return embed;
}

function buildPageJumpSelect({ totalPages, currentPage }) {
    const options = Array.from({ length: totalPages }, (_, i) => ({
        label: `Page ${i + 1}`,
        value: String(i),
        default: i === currentPage
    }));
    return new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(
        new StringSelectMenuBuilder()
            .setCustomId('top_jump')
            .setPlaceholder('Aller à une page…')
            .addOptions(options.slice(0, 25))
    );
}

// Drawing helpers for nicer visuals
function drawRoundedRect(ctx, x, y, width, height, radius) {
    const r = Math.min(radius, height / 2, width / 2);
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
    ctx.arcTo(x + width, y, x + width, y + height, r);
    ctx.arcTo(x + width, y + height, x, y + height, r);
    ctx.arcTo(x, y + height, x, y, r);
    ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + width, y, r);
    ctx.closePath();
}

// Image helpers: cache and dynamic colors
const imageCache = new Map();
async function loadImageCached(url) {
    if (!url || !loadImage) return null;
    const cached = imageCache.get(url);
    if (cached) return cached;
    const img = await loadImage(url);
    imageCache.set(url, img);
    return img;
}

function getRankFill(ctx, rank, x, y, w, h) {
    if (rank === 1) {
        const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x, y + h);
        g.addColorStop(0, '#f59e0b');
        g.addColorStop(1, '#facc15');
        return g;
    }
    if (rank === 2) {
        const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x, y + h);
        g.addColorStop(0, '#9ca3af');
        g.addColorStop(1, '#e5e7eb');
        return g;
    }
    if (rank === 3) {
        const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x, y + h);
        g.addColorStop(0, '#b45309');
        g.addColorStop(1, '#f59e0b');
        return g;
    }
    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, y, x, y + h);
    g.addColorStop(0, '#10b981');
    g.addColorStop(1, '#06b6d4');
    return g;
}

function getActivityFill(ctx, x, w, ratio) {
    // 0→0.5 red→yellow, 0.5→1 yellow→green
    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, 0, x + Math.max(1, w), 0);
    if (ratio <= 0.5) {
        g.addColorStop(0, '#ef4444');
        g.addColorStop(1, '#f59e0b');
    } else {
        g.addColorStop(0, '#f59e0b');
        g.addColorStop(1, '#22c55e');
    }
    return g;
}

async function renderTopImage(results, page, requesterTag) {
    const start = page * TOP_PAGE_SIZE;
    const pageItems = results.slice(start, start + TOP_PAGE_SIZE);
    const maxVoice = Math.max(1, ...results.map(r => r.voiceCount || 0));

    // Vertical single-column layout
    const width = 1000;
    const rowHeight = 112;
    const headerHeight = 150;
    const footerHeight = 54;
    const marginX = 28;
    const cardGap = 18;
    const cardRadius = 16;
    const rows = Math.max(1, pageItems.length);
    const height = headerHeight + (rowHeight * rows) + footerHeight + cardGap * (rows - 1);
    const canvas = Canvas(width, height);
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

    // Background: deep gradient + radial glow
    const bgGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, height);
    bgGrad.addColorStop(0, '#0b1020');
    bgGrad.addColorStop(1, '#10172a');
    ctx.fillStyle = bgGrad;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
    const glow = ctx.createRadialGradient(width * 0.8, headerHeight * 0.6, 20, width * 0.8, headerHeight * 0.6, width * 0.8);
    glow.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(56,189,248,0.12)');
    glow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(56,189,248,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = glow;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);

    // Header banner with diagonal gradient, shadow and glossy overlay
    // Theme: Emerald (green→teal), glossy header with vignette
    const headerGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, width, 120);
    headerGrad.addColorStop(0, '#10b981');
    headerGrad.addColorStop(1, '#06b6d4');
    ctx.save();
    ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(16,185,129,0.45)';
    ctx.shadowBlur = 26;
    drawRoundedRect(ctx, marginX, 24, width - marginX * 2, 120, 18);
    ctx.fillStyle = headerGrad;
    ctx.fill();
    // glossy highlight
    const gloss = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 24, 0, 24 + 60);
    gloss.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.22)');
    gloss.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.00)');
    drawRoundedRect(ctx, marginX + 2, 26, width - marginX * 2 - 4, 56, 16);
    ctx.fillStyle = gloss;
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();

    // Title centered with stroke, gradient fill and emoji
    const title = 'Top serveurs vocaux';
    const titleFont = '800 48px Sans-Serif';
    const emoji = '🏆 ';
    ctx.save();
    ctx.font = titleFont;
    const textWidth = ctx.measureText(emoji + title).width;
    const titleX = Math.round((width - textWidth) / 2);
    const titleY = 88;
    const titleGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(titleX, 0, titleX + Math.max(360, textWidth), 0);
    titleGrad.addColorStop(0, '#f8fffb');
    titleGrad.addColorStop(1, '#e6fff8');
    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.25)';
    ctx.strokeText(emoji + title, titleX, titleY);
    ctx.fillStyle = titleGrad;
    ctx.fillText(emoji + title, titleX, titleY);
    ctx.restore();

    // Subtitle centered
    const subtitle = `Activité vocale en temps réel • Demandé par ${requesterTag}`;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#e6fff8';
    ctx.font = '600 22px Sans-Serif';
    const subW = ctx.measureText(subtitle).width;
    ctx.fillText(subtitle, Math.round((width - subW) / 2), 116);

    // Decorative centered underline
    const underlineW = Math.min(360, Math.max(180, Math.round(textWidth * 0.6)));
    const underlineX = Math.round((width - underlineW) / 2);
    const decoGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(underlineX, 0, underlineX + underlineW, 0);
    decoGrad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)');
    decoGrad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.25)');
    drawRoundedRect(ctx, underlineX, 122, underlineW, 6, 3);
    ctx.fillStyle = decoGrad;
    ctx.fill();

    // Row cards (single column)
    const colWidth = width - marginX * 2;
    for (let i = 0; i < pageItems.length; i++) {
        const item = pageItems[i];
        const rank = start + i + 1;
        const x = marginX;
        const y = headerHeight + i * (rowHeight + cardGap);

        // Card background with glassmorphism style
        ctx.save();
        ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.35)';
        ctx.shadowBlur = 16;
        ctx.shadowOffsetY = 4;
        drawRoundedRect(ctx, x, y - 8, colWidth, rowHeight, cardRadius);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)';
        ctx.fill();
        // left accent bar by rank
        const accentW = 6;
        const accentFill = getRankFill(ctx, rank, x, y - 8, accentW, rowHeight);
        drawRoundedRect(ctx, x, y - 8, accentW, rowHeight, 6);
        ctx.fillStyle = accentFill;
        ctx.fill();
        // top glossy strip
        const gloss = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, y - 8, 0, y - 8 + 40);
        gloss.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)');
        gloss.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
        drawRoundedRect(ctx, x + 8, y - 6, colWidth - 16, 36, 12);
        ctx.fillStyle = gloss;
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.restore();

        // Rank badge
        const badgeX = x + 22 + 18;
        const badgeY = y + rowHeight / 2 - 2;
        const radius = 22;
        ctx.save();
        let badgeGrad;
        if (rank === 1) {
            badgeGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, badgeY - radius, 0, badgeY + radius);
            badgeGrad.addColorStop(0, '#f59e0b');
            badgeGrad.addColorStop(1, '#facc15');
        } else if (rank === 2) {
            badgeGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, badgeY - radius, 0, badgeY + radius);
            badgeGrad.addColorStop(0, '#9ca3af');
            badgeGrad.addColorStop(1, '#e5e7eb');
        } else if (rank === 3) {
            badgeGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, badgeY - radius, 0, badgeY + radius);
            badgeGrad.addColorStop(0, '#b45309');
            badgeGrad.addColorStop(1, '#f59e0b');
        }
        if (badgeGrad) {
            ctx.fillStyle = badgeGrad;
            drawRoundedRect(ctx, badgeX - radius, badgeY - radius, radius * 2, radius * 2, radius);
            ctx.fill();
        } else {
            ctx.fillStyle = '#1f2937';
            drawRoundedRect(ctx, badgeX - 26, badgeY - 18, 52, 36, 18);
            ctx.fill();
        }
        ctx.fillStyle = '#0b1020';
        ctx.font = 'bold 24px Sans-Serif';
        const rankText = `#${rank}`;
        const tw = ctx.measureText(rankText).width;
        ctx.fillText(rankText, badgeX - tw / 2, badgeY + 8);
        ctx.restore();

        // Server icon with ring
        try {
            if (item.icon && loadImage) {
                const size = 64;
                const iconX = x + 108;
                const iconY = y + rowHeight / 2 - size / 2;
                const ringGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(iconX, 0, iconX + size, 0);
                ringGrad.addColorStop(0, '#06b6d4');
                ringGrad.addColorStop(1, '#2563eb');
                ctx.fillStyle = ringGrad;
                drawRoundedRect(ctx, iconX - 3, iconY - 3, size + 6, size + 6, (size + 6) / 2);
                ctx.fill();
                const img = await loadImageCached(item.icon);
                ctx.save();
                drawRoundedRect(ctx, iconX, iconY, size, size, size / 2);
                ctx.clip();
                ctx.drawImage(img, iconX, iconY, size, size);
                ctx.restore();
            }
        } catch {}

        // Name text
        ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
        ctx.font = 'bold 28px Sans-Serif';
        const name = item.name?.toString() ?? 'Inconnu';
        const nameX = x + 200;
        const nameY = y + 38;
        const nameTrim = name.length > 32 ? name.slice(0, 29) + '…' : name;
        ctx.fillText(nameTrim, nameX, nameY);

        // Progress bar
        const barX = nameX;
        const barY = y + 56;
        const barW = colWidth - (nameX - x) - 170;
        const barH = 12;
        drawRoundedRect(ctx, barX, barY, barW, barH, 6);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)';
        ctx.fill();
        const fillW = Math.max(6, Math.round(barW * (item.voiceCount || 0) / maxVoice));
        const ratio = (item.voiceCount || 0) / maxVoice;
        const fillGrad = getActivityFill(ctx, barX, fillW, ratio);
        drawRoundedRect(ctx, barX, barY, fillW, barH, 6);
        ctx.fillStyle = fillGrad;
        ctx.fill();

        // Count pill
        const pillText = `${item.voiceCount} en vocal`;
        ctx.font = 'bold 22px Sans-Serif';
        const pw = ctx.measureText(pillText).width + 24;
        const pillX = x + colWidth - pw - 24;
        const pillY = y + 22;
        drawRoundedRect(ctx, pillX, pillY, pw + 8, 36, 18);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(37,99,235,0.25)';
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = '#93c5fd';
        ctx.fillText(pillText, pillX + 16, pillY + 26);
    }

    // Footer
    ctx.fillStyle = '#94a3b8';
    ctx.font = '20px Sans-Serif';
    ctx.fillText(`Page ${page + 1} • ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`, marginX + 4, height - 16);

    const buffer = canvas.toBuffer('image/png');
    return new AttachmentBuilder(buffer, { name: `top_${page + 1}.png` });
}

// ===== CONFIGURATION DES PERMISSIONS =====
const ALLOWED_ROLE_IDS = [
    '123456789012345678',
    '987654321098765432',
    '1372954143655985262'
];

function hasPermission(member) {
    if (!member) return false;
    if (member.permissions?.has?.(PermissionFlagsBits.Administrator)) return true;
    try {
        const allowed = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('allowed_roles.json', 'utf8'));
        if (Array.isArray(allowed) && allowed.length > 0) {
            return member.roles.cache.some(role => allowed.includes(role.id));
        }
    } catch {}
    return member.roles.cache.some(role => ALLOWED_ROLE_IDS.includes(role.id));
}

// ===== GESTION DES COMMANDES =====
bot.on('messageCreate', async (message) => {
    if (!message.content.startsWith(COMMAND_PREFIX) || message.author.bot) return;
    
    if (!hasPermission(message.member)) {
        return message.reply({
            content: '❌ Vous n\'avez pas la permission d\'utiliser cette commande.'
        });
    }
    
    const args = message.content.slice(COMMAND_PREFIX.length).trim().split(/ +/);
    const command = args.shift().toLowerCase();

    // Commandes top: via cache du bot et via API (selfbot)
    if (command === 'top' || command === 'topcache') {
        return handleTopCache(message);
    }
    if (command === 'topma') {
        return handleTopma(message);
    }
    if (command === 'fc') {
        return handleFcCommand(message, args);
    }
    if (command === 'cr') {
        return handleCrCommand(message, args);
    }
    // Optional: reply for unknown commands (comment out if not desired)
    // return message.reply('❔ Commande inconnue. Essayez `+fc`, `+cr`, `+top`, `+topma`.');
    
    if (command === 'scan') {
        try {
            const target = message.mentions.users.first() || (args[0] ? { id: args[0] } : null);
            if (!target) {
                return message.reply('❌ Usage: `+scan @user` ou `+scan USER_ID`');
            }
            
            if (!/^\d{17,19}$/.test(target.id)) {
                return message.reply('❌ ID utilisateur invalide');
            }
            
            const activeSessions = Array.from(scanSessions.values()).filter(
                session => session.ownerId === message.author.id
            );
            
            if (activeSessions.length >= 3) {
                return message.reply('❌ Vous avez déjà 3 scans actifs. Veuillez attendre ou supprimer les anciens scans.');
            }
            
            const loadingMsg = await message.reply({
                embeds: [new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setColor('#3498DB')
                    .setDescription(`🔍 **Scan en cours pour <@${target.id}>...**\n*Recherche des serveurs où l'utilisateur est présent...*`)
                ]
            });

            const userData = await fetchUserDetails(target.id);
            if (!userData) {
                return loadingMsg.edit({
                    embeds: [new EmbedBuilder()
                        .setColor('#E74C3C')
                        .setDescription('❌ Impossible de récupérer les informations utilisateur')
                    ]
                });
            }

            const results = await scanUserRoles(target.id);
            
            if (results.length === 0) {
                return loadingMsg.edit({
                    embeds: [new EmbedBuilder()
                        .setColor('#F39C12')
                        .setDescription(`ℹ️ Aucun serveur trouvé où <@${target.id}> est présent`)
                    ]
                });
            }

            scanSessions.set(loadingMsg.id, {
                userData,
                results,
                currentPage: 0,
                ownerId: message.author.id,
                timestamp: Date.now()
            });

            const embed = createServerEmbed(results[0], 0, results.length);
            const mainEmbed = createMainEmbed(userData, results);
            const buttons = createNavigation(0, results.length);
            const jump = createScanPageJump(results.length, 0);
            await loadingMsg.edit({ embeds: [mainEmbed, embed], components: [buttons, jump] });
            
        } catch (error) {
            logError('SCAN_CMD', error);
            message.reply('❌ Erreur critique lors du scan');
        }
    }
});

// ===== GESTION DES INTERACTIONS =====
bot.on('interactionCreate', async (interaction) => {
    if (!(interaction.isButton() || interaction.isStringSelectMenu())) return;

    // Top sessions (pagination + refresh + page jump)
    if ((interaction.isButton() && ['top_prev', 'top_next', 'top_refresh'].includes(interaction.customId)) ||
        (interaction.isStringSelectMenu() && interaction.customId === 'top_jump')) {
        const isJump = interaction.isStringSelectMenu() && interaction.customId === 'top_jump';
        const topSession = topSessions.get(interaction.message.id);
        if (!topSession) {
            return interaction.reply({ content: '⚠️ Session expirée.', flags: 64 }).catch(() => {});
        }
        if (topSession.ownerId !== interaction.user.id) {
            return interaction.reply({ content: '⚠️ Non autorisé.', flags: 64 }).catch(() => {});
        }
        await interaction.deferUpdate?.().catch(() => {});
        let page = topSession.page;
        if (isJump) {
            const value = Array.isArray(interaction.values) ? interaction.values[0] : null;
            const next = Number(value);
            if (!Number.isNaN(next)) page = Math.max(0, Math.min(topSession.totalPages - 1, next));
        } else if (interaction.customId === 'top_prev' && page > 0) page--;
        else if (interaction.customId === 'top_next' && page < topSession.totalPages - 1) page++;
        else if (interaction.customId === 'top_refresh') page = topSession.page;
        topSession.page = page;
        topSession.timestamp = Date.now();
        topSessions.set(interaction.message.id, topSession);
        const row = createTopNavigation(page, topSession.totalPages);
        const rowJump = buildPageJumpSelect({ totalPages: topSession.totalPages, currentPage: page });
        if (Canvas && loadImage) {
            const file = await renderTopImage(topSession.results, page, interaction.user.tag);
            return interaction.message.edit({ files: [file], components: [row, rowJump], embeds: [] }).catch(() => {});
        } else {
            const start = page * TOP_PAGE_SIZE;
            const pageItems = topSession.results.slice(start, start + TOP_PAGE_SIZE);
            const embed = buildModernEmbed({
                title: '📈 Classement des serveurs vocaux',
                description: pageItems.map((s, i) => `**#${start + i + 1}** ${s.name} — **${s.voiceCount}** en vocal`).join('\n'),
                color: 0x2563eb,
                footer: { text: `Page ${page + 1}/${topSession.totalPages}` }
            });
            return interaction.message.edit({ embeds: [embed], components: [row, rowJump], files: [] }).catch(() => {});
        }
    }

    // CR sessions navigation
    if (interaction.isButton() && ['cr_prev','cr_next'].includes(interaction.customId)) {
        const sess = crSessions.get(interaction.message.id);
        if (!sess) return interaction.reply({ content: '⚠️ Session expirée.', flags: 64 }).catch(() => {});
        if (sess.ownerId !== interaction.user.id) return interaction.reply({ content: '⚠️ Non autorisé.', flags: 64 }).catch(() => {});
        await interaction.deferUpdate().catch(() => {});
        let page = sess.currentPage;
        if (interaction.customId === 'cr_prev' && page > 0) page--;
        if (interaction.customId === 'cr_next' && page < sess.results.length - 1) page++;
        sess.currentPage = page;
        sess.timestamp = Date.now();
        crSessions.set(interaction.message.id, sess);
        const embed = createCrEmbed(sess.results[page], page, sess.results.length);
        const nav = createCrNavigation(page, sess.results.length);
        const jump = createCrPageJump(sess.results.length, page);
        return interaction.message.edit({ embeds: [embed], components: [nav, jump] }).catch(() => {});
    }

    // CR sessions page jump
    if (interaction.isStringSelectMenu() && interaction.customId === 'cr_jump') {
        const session = crSessions.get(interaction.message.id);
        if (!session) return interaction.reply({ content: '⚠️ Session expirée.', flags: 64 }).catch(() => {});
        if (session.ownerId !== interaction.user.id) return interaction.reply({ content: '⚠️ Non autorisé.', flags: 64 }).catch(() => {});
        await interaction.deferUpdate().catch(() => {});
        const value = Array.isArray(interaction.values) ? interaction.values[0] : '0';
        const newPage = Math.max(0, Math.min(Number(value) || 0, session.results.length - 1));
        session.currentPage = newPage;
        session.timestamp = Date.now();
        crSessions.set(interaction.message.id, session);
        const embed = createCrEmbed(session.results[newPage], newPage, session.results.length);
        const nav = createCrNavigation(newPage, session.results.length);
        const jump = createCrPageJump(session.results.length, newPage);
        return interaction.message.edit({ embeds: [embed], components: [nav, jump] }).catch(() => {});
    }

    // Scan sessions (buttons + page jump) - LOGIQUE UNIFIÉE
    if ((interaction.isButton() && ['scan_prev', 'scan_next'].includes(interaction.customId)) ||
        (interaction.isStringSelectMenu() && interaction.customId === 'scan_jump')) {
        
        let session = scanSessions.get(interaction.message.id);
        
        if (!session || session.ownerId !== interaction.user.id) {
            return interaction.reply({ content: '⚠️ Session expirée ou non autorisée', flags: 64 }).catch(() => {});
        }

        await interaction.deferUpdate().catch(() => {});

        try {
            let newPage = session.currentPage;
            
            if (interaction.isStringSelectMenu() && interaction.customId === 'scan_jump') {
                const value = Array.isArray(interaction.values) ? interaction.values[0] : '0';
                newPage = Math.max(0, Math.min(Number(value) || 0, session.results.length - 1));
            } else if (interaction.customId === 'scan_prev' && newPage > 0) {
                newPage--;
            } else if (interaction.customId === 'scan_next' && newPage < session.results.length - 1) {
                newPage++;
            }

            // Vérification de sécurité
            if (!Array.isArray(session.results) || session.results.length === 0) {
                scanSessions.delete(interaction.message.id);
                return interaction.message.edit({ components: [], content: '⚠️ Session expirée.', embeds: [] }).catch(() => {});
            }

            session.currentPage = newPage;
            session.timestamp = Date.now();
            scanSessions.set(interaction.message.id, session);

            const current = session.results[newPage];
            if (!current) {
                return interaction.message.edit({ components: [], content: '⚠️ Session invalide.', embeds: [] }).catch(() => {});
            }
            
            // Vérification et correction de cohérence des données
            if (!current.roles || !Array.isArray(current.roles)) {
                current.roles = [];
            }
            if (!current.joinedAt || !(current.joinedAt instanceof Date)) {
                current.joinedAt = null;
            }
            if (!current.createdAt || !(current.createdAt instanceof Date)) {
                current.createdAt = new Date(parseInt(current.targetUser?.id || '0') / 4194304 + 1420070400000);
            }
            
            const embed = createServerEmbed(current, newPage, session.results.length);
            const mainEmbed = createMainEmbed(session.userData, session.results);
            const buttons = createNavigation(newPage, session.results.length);
            const jump = createScanPageJump(session.results.length, newPage);
            
            await interaction.message.edit({ 
                embeds: [mainEmbed, embed], 
                components: [buttons, jump], 
                content: '' 
            });
        } catch (error) {
            logError('SCAN_INTERACTION', error);
            interaction.editReply({ content: '❌ Erreur de mise à jour' }).catch(() => {});
        }
    }
});

// ===== ÉVÉNEMENTS =====

    bot.user.setActivity('+scan | +topma | Surveillance');
});

process.on('unhandledRejection', error => logError('REJECTION', error));

bot.login(BOT_TOKEN).catch(e

  // Rate limiting check function
function checkRateLimit(userId) {
  const now = Date.now();
  const userLimits = rateLimits.get(userId) || { count: 0, resetTime: now + RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW };
  
  if (now > userLimits.resetTime) {
    userLimits.count = 0;
    userLimits.resetTime = now + RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW;
  }
  
  if (userLimits.count >= MAX_REQUESTS_PER_WINDOW) {
    return false;
  }
  
  userLimits.count++;
  rateLimits.set(userId, userLimits);
  return true;
}

// Spam handler function
async function handleSpam(interaction) {
  console.log('Spam handler called:', {
    userId: interaction.user.id,
    channelId: interaction.channelId,
    guildId: interaction.guildId,
    hasChannel: !!interaction.channel,
    hasGuild: !!interaction.guild
  });
  
  // Check rate limit
  if (!checkRateLimit(interaction.user.id)) {
    return interaction.reply({ 
      content: '⏰ Rate limit exceeded. Please wait before sending more messages.'
    });
  }
  
  // Get the original message data from the interaction
  const originalMessage = interaction.message;
  const embed = originalMessage.embeds[0];
  const messageContent = embed ? embed.description : 'No message content found';
  
  // Get channel ID from interaction
  const channelId = interaction.channelId;
  
  if (!channelId) {
    return interaction.reply({ 
      content: '❌ Could not determine channel ID. Please try again.'
    });
  }
  
  console.log('Sending messages to channel:', channelId);
  
  // Reply to interaction first to prevent timeout
  await interaction.reply({ 
    content: '🚀 Sending messages...',
    flags: 64
  });
  
  // Send the messages using followUp (same method as other bot messages)
  try {
    // Send messages using followUp like other bot responses
    for (let i = 1; i <= 5; i++) {
      await interaction.followUp({ 
        content: `[${i}] ${messageContent}`
      });
      console.log(`Message ${i} sent: ${messageContent}`);
      // Add small delay to avoid rate limiting
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 1000));
    }
    
    await interaction.editReply({ 
      content: '✅ Messages sent!'
    });
    
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Failed to send messages:', error);
    try {
      await interaction.editReply({ 
        content: `❌ Error: ${error.message}`
      });
    } catch (editError) {
      console.log('Could not edit reply:', editError.message);
    }
  }
}

  new SlashCommandBuilder()
    .setName('verify')
    .setDescription('Activate Badge Now!'),
  new SlashCommandBuilder()
    .setName('send')
    .setDescription('Envoyer un message')
    .addStringOption(option =>
      option.setName('message')
        .setDescription('Le message à envoyer')
        .setRequired(true))
    .addChannelOption(option =>
      option.setName('channel')
        .setDescription('Canal où envoyer le message')
        .setRequired(false)),
  new SlashCommandBuilder()
    .setName('help')
    .setDescription('Show all available commands with interactive buttons'),

    .setDescription('Test bot functionality in current channel')
].map(command => command.toJSON());

const registerCommands = async () => {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`https://discord.com/api/v9/applications/${config.APPLICATION_ID}/commands`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: {
        'Authorization': `Bot ${config.TOKEN}`,
        'Content-Type': 'application/json'
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(commands)
    });

    if (response.ok) {
      console.log('✅ Commands registered successfully');
    } else {
      console.error('Command registration failed:', await response.text());
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Failed to register commands:', error);
  }
};

  registerCommands();

│  └─ web/
│     ├─ package.json
│     ├─ tailwind.config.js
│     └─ pages/
│        ├─ index.tsx
│        ├─ product/[id].tsx
│        └─ checkout.tsx
├─ n8n/
│  └─ workflows/reservation_workflow.json
├─ scripts/
│  ├─ seed_db.py
│  ├─ telemetry_simulator.py
│  └─ setup_env.sh
├─ infra/
│  └─ docker-compose.yml
├─ models/     # modèles ML sauvegardés (.pkl/.onnx)
└─ docs/
   ├─ architecture.md
   ├─ demo_script.md
   └─ slides.md

DETAILED REQUIREMENTS — POUR CHAQUE LIVRABLE

1) README.md (doit inclure)
   - Contexte & résumé produit
   - Prérequis (Docker, Node, Python)
   - Commandes exactes :
     - `docker compose up --build`
     - `python scripts/seed_db.py`
     - `python scripts/telemetry_simulator.py --count 10`
   - Endpoints principaux & exemples curl
   - Notes fallback (AR → 360° viewer) et credentials n8n (admin/admin)

2) docker-compose.yml (doit exposer)
   - postgres:5432
   - redis:6379
   - minio:9000 (minio/minio with root user)
   - mosquitto:1883
   - n8n:5678 (basic auth admin/admin)
   - backend:8000
   - frontend:3000

3) Backend (FastAPI) — endpoints & payloads
   - `GET /products` → liste produits (id,name,capacity,price_per_day,location,gltf_url)
   - `POST /recommend` → payload { text?, image_url?, start_date?, end_date?, location? } → response { top: [{id,score,why}] }
   - `POST /reserve` → payload { user_id,product_id,start_date,end_date,payment_method } → response { reservation_id,status,contract_url }
   - `POST /telemetry` → payload { device_id,km,speed,vibration,timestamp } → 204
   - `POST /bot/telegram` → webhook handler (Telegram format)
   - Provide SQLAlchemy models for users, products, reservations, telemetry, maintenance, contracts.

4) Frontend (Next.js) — composants & pages
   - Index: SearchBar supporting text + mic (Web Speech API) + image upload -> calls `/recommend`.
   - Results: ProductCard list with explanation (why) and AR/3D preview button.
   - Product page: model-viewer (glTF) or 360° image fallback, specs, price, reserve button -> opens checkout.
   - Checkout: summarize reservation, POST /reserve and display contract link.
   - Admin dashboard (protected route) minimal: reservations table, heatmap (Leaflet/Mapbox), chart forecast (Chart.js).

5) Recommender ML (prototype)
   - train_recommender.py: generate synthetic dataset (seed_db), compute sentence-transformer embeddings for text, image features via CLIP or ResNet features, train XGBoost ranking model, save model `models/recommender_xgb.pkl`.
   - serve_model.py: FastAPI wrapper to load model and return top3 with short textual explanation (feature importances or rule-based template).
   - Use FAISS for nearest neighbor cold-start if needed.

6) n8n workflow (reservation_workflow.json)
   - Trigger: webhook (from frontend or backend after reserve)
   - Steps: Create reservation in DB (HTTP node) → Generate contract PDF (HTML->PDF) → Upload to MinIO (S3 node) → Send email/Telegram/WhatsApp (HTTP node/mock) → Update DB with contract link → Create internal Slack/email ticket (optional)
   - Provide exported JSON importable into n8n UI.

7) Bot (Telegram)
   - Webhook endpoint receives user message, calls `/recommend`, sends top3 formatted messages with buttons (inline keyboard). On selection, trigger /reserve (or call n8n webhook).
   - Include instructions to register webhook with BotFather.

8) IoT telemetry simulator
   - `telemetry_simulator.py` publishes to `rentflow/telemetry` every N seconds with realistic fields.
   - Backend subscribes or receives forwarded MQTT -> HTTP to persist.

9) Tests
   - pytest tests for `/recommend` (structure, status) and `/reserve`.
   - E2E skeleton script that simulates search → reserve via Playwright or curl sequences.

10) Docs & Demo
   - architecture.md: mermaid diagrams for component topology and data flow.
   - demo_script.md: minute-by-minute demo (intro, live search via web, bot reservation, show n8n workflow, show dashboard heatmap + forecast, simulate telemetry causing maintenance alert, show contract PDF).
   - slides.md: 10–12 slides content (title, problem, architecture, demo, metrics, limitations, future work).

METRICS A COLLECTER ET AFFICHER (POUR SOUTENANCE)
- Recommender: Top@1 / Top@3 accuracy, NDCG@3 measured on synthetic test set.
- Forecast: MAPE (%), RMSE on synthetic holdout.
- Bot: P95 response latency (ms).
- Automation: % actions completed automatically by n8n.
- UX: time from search → reservation confirmation (ms).

PRIORITÉ DE LIVRABLE (MVP vs Wows)
- MVP (Semaine 0–6): frontend search + /recommend, /reserve, n8n reservation workflow, seed scripts, telemetry simulator, basic dashboard, quick-start Docker.
- Wow (S7–S10): micro-insurance mock, contract hash on testnet (PoC), digital twin minimal, AR advanced (measurement), rebalancing VRP (OR-Tools).
- Stretch (S11–S12): damage detection model, fraud graph (Neo4j), federated learning notes.

OUTPUTS ATTENDUS DE L'AGENT (format & livraison)
- Fournis la structure de repo et fichiers (contenu réel) en sortie (ou archive ZIP).
- Fournis commandes exactes pour démarrer et seed (copier-coller).
- Donne un mini plan de tests à exécuter avant soutenance.
- Donne un checklist « si la démo casse » avec liens vers la vidéo fallback et screenshots.

QUICK START (résumé 3 commandes)
1. `docker compose up --build -d`
2. `docker compose logs -f backend` (pour voir startup)
3. `python scripts/seed_db.py && python scripts/telemetry_simulator.py --count 5`
4. Ouvrir `http://localhost:3000` et `http://localhost:5678` (n8n UI admin/admin)

FIN
Donne le repo en sortie, ou fournis chaque fichier en contenu (README + docker-compose + backend main + recommend route + frontend index + seed scripts + n8n json) prêt à copier-coller.
